import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:lucide_icons/lucide_icons.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/api_client.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/osi_repository.dart';

// Дома и жители (REQ-137/143 §8: онбординг председателя доводится до «добавления жителей»,
// но это ПОСТОЯННАЯ функция, не разовый шаг визарда — председатель возвращается сюда всегда).
class ApartmentsResidentsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const ApartmentsResidentsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<ApartmentsResidentsScreen> createState() => _ApartmentsResidentsScreenState();
}

class _ApartmentsResidentsScreenState extends State<ApartmentsResidentsScreen> {
  List<Building> _buildings = [];
  Building? _selected;
  List<Apartment> _apartments = [];
  bool _loading = true;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadBuildings();
  }

  Future<void> _loadBuildings() async {
    setState(() {
      _loading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final buildings = await OsiRepository.instance.listBuildings();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _buildings = buildings;
        _selected = buildings.isNotEmpty ? buildings.first : null;
        _loading = false;
      });
      if (_selected != null) await _loadApartments();
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _error = e.toString();
        _loading = false;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _loadApartments() async {
    if (_selected == null) return;
    try {
      final apartments = await OsiRepository.instance.listApartments(_selected!.id);
      if (mounted) setState(() => _apartments = apartments);
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString())));
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _addBuilding() async {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    final addressCtrl = TextEditingController();
    final added = await showModalBottomSheet<bool>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: c.card,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(AppDimens.radiusLg)),
      ),
      builder: (_) => _SimpleFormSheet(
        title: 'Новый дом',
        fields: [_SheetField('Адрес', addressCtrl, LucideIcons.home)],
        submitLabel: 'Добавить',
        onSubmit: () => OsiRepository.instance.createBuilding(address: addressCtrl.text.trim()),
        validate: () => addressCtrl.text.trim().length >= 3 ? null : 'Укажите адрес',
      ),
    );
    if (added == true) await _loadBuildings();
  }

  Future<void> _addApartment() async {
    if (_selected == null) return;
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    final numberCtrl = TextEditingController();
    final added = await showModalBottomSheet<bool>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: c.card,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(AppDimens.radiusLg)),
      ),
      builder: (_) => _SimpleFormSheet(
        title: 'Новая квартира',
        fields: [_SheetField('Номер квартиры', numberCtrl, LucideIcons.doorOpen)],
        submitLabel: 'Добавить',
        onSubmit: () =>
            OsiRepository.instance.createApartment(buildingId: _selected!.id, number: numberCtrl.text.trim()),
        validate: () => numberCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty ? 'Укажите номер' : null,
      ),
    );
    if (added == true) await _loadApartments();
  }

  Future<void> _addResident(Apartment apt) async {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    final nameCtrl = TextEditingController();
    final phoneCtrl = TextEditingController();
    final emailCtrl = TextEditingController();
    await showModalBottomSheet<bool>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: c.card,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(AppDimens.radiusLg)),
      ),
      builder: (_) => _SimpleFormSheet(
        title: 'Житель кв. ${apt.number}',
        fields: [
          _SheetField('ФИО', nameCtrl, LucideIcons.user),
          _SheetField('Телефон', phoneCtrl, LucideIcons.phone),
          _SheetField('Email', emailCtrl, LucideIcons.atSign),
        ],
        submitLabel: 'Добавить жителя',
        onSubmit: () => OsiRepository.instance.addResident(
          apartmentId: apt.id,
          fullName: nameCtrl.text.trim(),
          phone: phoneCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty ? null : phoneCtrl.text.trim(),
          email: emailCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty ? null : emailCtrl.text.trim(),
        ),
        validate: () {
          if (nameCtrl.text.trim().length < 2) return 'Укажите ФИО';
          if (phoneCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty && emailCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty) {
            return 'Укажите телефон или email';
          }
          return null;
        },
      ),
    );
    if (mounted) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('Житель добавлен')));
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: c.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: c.background,
        elevation: 0,
        title: Text('Дома и жители', style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary)),
        iconTheme: IconThemeData(color: c.textPrimary),
      ),
      body: _loading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : _error != null
              ? Center(
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceLg),
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Text(_error!, style: TextStyle(color: c.danger), textAlign: TextAlign.center),
                        const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
                        OutlinedButton(onPressed: _loadBuildings, child: const Text('Повторить')),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                )
              : _buildings.isEmpty
                  ? Center(
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceLg),
                        child: Column(
                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                          children: [
                            Text('Домов пока нет', style: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary)),
                            const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
                            ElevatedButton(
                              onPressed: _addBuilding,
                              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: c.primary),
                              child: const Text('Добавить дом', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    )
                  : Column(
                      children: [
                        Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
                          child: Row(
                            children: [
                              Expanded(
                                child: DropdownButtonFormField<Building>(
                                  initialValue: _selected,
                                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                                    filled: true,
                                    fillColor: c.surfaceVariant,
                                    contentPadding:
                                        const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
                                    border: OutlineInputBorder(
                                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
                                        borderSide: BorderSide.none),
                                  ),
                                  items: _buildings
                                      .map((b) => DropdownMenuItem(value: b, child: Text(b.address)))
                                      .toList(),
                                  onChanged: (b) async {
                                    setState(() {
                                      _selected = b;
                                      _apartments = [];
                                    });
                                    await _loadApartments();
                                  },
                                ),
                              ),
                              IconButton(
                                onPressed: _addBuilding,
                                icon: Icon(Icons.add_business, color: c.primary),
                                tooltip: 'Добавить дом',
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                        Expanded(
                          child: RefreshIndicator(
                            onRefresh: _loadApartments,
                            child: _apartments.isEmpty
                                ? Center(
                                    child: Text('Квартир пока нет',
                                        style: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary)),
                                  )
                                : ListView.separated(
                                    padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                                        AppDimens.spaceMd, 0, AppDimens.spaceMd, 80),
                                    itemCount: _apartments.length,
                                    separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
                                    itemBuilder: (_, i) {
                                      final apt = _apartments[i];
                                      return Container(
                                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
                                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                          color: c.card,
                                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
                                          border: Border.all(color: c.cardBorder),
                                        ),
                                        child: Row(
                                          children: [
                                            Icon(LucideIcons.doorOpen, color: c.primary),
                                            const SizedBox(width: AppDimens.spaceSm),
                                            Expanded(
                                              child: Text('Квартира ${apt.number}',
                                                  style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary)),
                                            ),
                                            TextButton.icon(
                                              onPressed: () => _addResident(apt),
                                              icon: Icon(LucideIcons.userPlus, size: 16, color: c.primary),
                                              label: Text('Житель', style: TextStyle(color: c.primary)),
                                            ),
                                          ],
                                        ),
                                      );
                                    },
                                  ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
      floatingActionButton: _buildings.isNotEmpty
          ? FloatingActionButton(
              onPressed: _addApartment,
              backgroundColor: c.primary,
              child: const Icon(Icons.add, color: Colors.white),
            )
          : null,
    );
  }
}

class _SheetField {
  final String label;
  final TextEditingController controller;
  final IconData icon;
  _SheetField(this.label, this.controller, this.icon);
}

class _SimpleFormSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  final String title;
  final List<_SheetField> fields;
  final String submitLabel;
  final Future<void> Function() onSubmit;
  final String? Function() validate;

  const _SimpleFormSheet({
    required this.title,
    required this.fields,
    required this.submitLabel,
    required this.onSubmit,
    required this.validate,
  });

  @override
  State<_SimpleFormSheet> createState() => _SimpleFormSheetState();
}

class _SimpleFormSheetState extends State<_SimpleFormSheet> {
  bool _isLoading = false;
  String? _error;

  Future<void> _submit() async {
    final validationError = widget.validate();
    if (validationError != null) {
      setState(() => _error = validationError);
      return;
    }
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      await widget.onSubmit();
      if (mounted) Navigator.pop(context, true);
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      setState(() => _error = e.message);
    } catch (_) {
      setState(() => _error = 'Не удалось сохранить');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    return Padding(
      padding: EdgeInsets.only(
        left: AppDimens.spaceLg,
        right: AppDimens.spaceLg,
        top: AppDimens.spaceLg,
        bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom + AppDimens.spaceLg,
      ),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(widget.title,
              style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: AppTypography.bold)),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
          for (final f in widget.fields) ...[
            TextField(
              controller: f.controller,
              style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary),
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                labelText: f.label,
                labelStyle: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary),
                prefixIcon: Icon(f.icon, color: c.primary, size: 19),
                filled: true,
                fillColor: c.surfaceVariant,
                border: OutlineInputBorder(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd), borderSide: BorderSide.none),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
          ],
          if (_error != null) ...[
            Text(_error!, style: TextStyle(color: c.danger)),
            const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
          ],
          SizedBox(
            width: double.infinity,
            height: AppDimens.primaryTapTarget,
            child: ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _submit,
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                backgroundColor: c.primary,
                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd)),
              ),
              child: _isLoading
                  ? const SizedBox(
                      width: 22, height: 22,
                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2.5))
                  : Text(widget.submitLabel,
                      style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: AppTypography.bold)),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

=== ФАЙЛ: lib\screens\chairman_announcements_screen.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'package:lucide_icons/lucide_icons.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/api_client.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/announcements_repository.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/osi_repository.dart';

// Объявления дома — председатель (REQ-143). Список + создание. MVP: берём ПЕРВЫЙ дом ОСИ
// (Q-005 — большинство ОСИ управляют одним домом); выбор дома при нескольких — за пределами
// R2, отмечено в build-plan-3.
class ChairmanAnnouncementsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const ChairmanAnnouncementsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<ChairmanAnnouncementsScreen> createState() => _ChairmanAnnouncementsScreenState();
}

class _ChairmanAnnouncementsScreenState extends State<ChairmanAnnouncementsScreen> {
  List<Announcement> _items = [];
  String? _buildingId;
  bool _loading = true;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    setState(() {
      _loading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final buildings = await OsiRepository.instance.listBuildings();
      final items = await AnnouncementsRepository.instance.list();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _buildingId = buildings.isNotEmpty ? buildings.first.id : null;
        _items = items;
        _loading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _error = e.toString();
        _loading = false;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _openCreate() async {
    if (_buildingId == null) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
          .showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('Сначала добавьте дом')));
      return;
    }
    final created = await showModalBottomSheet<bool>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.of(context).card,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(AppDimens.radiusLg)),
      ),
      builder: (_) => _CreateAnnouncementSheet(buildingId: _buildingId!),
    );
    if (created == true && mounted) _load();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: c.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: c.background,
        elevation: 0,
        title: Text('Объявления', style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary)),
        iconTheme: IconThemeData(color: c.textPrimary),
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton(
        onPressed: _openCreate,
        backgroundColor: c.primary,
        child: const Icon(Icons.add, color: Colors.white),
      ),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: _load,
        child: _loading
            ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
            : _error != null
                ? Center(
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceLg),
                      child: Column(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          Text(_error!, style: TextStyle(color: c.danger), textAlign: TextAlign.center),
                          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
                          OutlinedButton(onPressed: _load, child: const Text('Повторить')),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  )
                : _items.isEmpty
                    ? Center(
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceLg),
                          child: Text('Объявлений пока нет — нажмите «+», чтобы опубликовать первое',
                              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                              style: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary)),
                        ),
                      )
                    : ListView.separated(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                            AppDimens.spaceMd, AppDimens.spaceMd, AppDimens.spaceMd, 80),
                        itemCount: _items.length,
                        separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
                        itemBuilder: (_, i) {
                          final a = _items[i];
                          return Container(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: c.card,
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
                              border: Border.all(
                                  color: a.isPinned ? c.secondary.withOpacity(0.5) : c.cardBorder),
                            ),
                            child: Column(
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                              children: [
                                Row(
                                  children: [
                                    if (a.isPinned) ...[
                                      Icon(LucideIcons.pin, size: 14, color: c.secondary),
                                      const SizedBox(width: 6),
                                    ],
                                    Expanded(
                                      child: Text(a.title,
                                          style: TextStyle(
                                              color: c.textPrimary, fontWeight: AppTypography.semibold)),
                                    ),
                                    Text('прочитали: ${a.readCount}',
                                        style:
                                            TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary, fontSize: AppTypography.caption)),
                                  ],
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                                Text(a.body,
                                    maxLines: 2,
                                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                    style: TextStyle(color: c.textSecondary)),
                                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                                Text(DateFormat('d MMMM, HH:mm', 'ru').format(a.createdAt.toLocal()),
                                    style: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary, fontSize: AppTypography.caption)),
                              ],
                            ),
                          );
                        },
                      ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _CreateAnnouncementSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  final String buildingId;
  const _CreateAnnouncementSheet({required this.buildingId});

  @override
  State<_CreateAnnouncementSheet> createState() => _CreateAnnouncementSheetState();
}

class _CreateAnnouncementSheetState extends State<_CreateAnnouncementSheet> {
  final _titleCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _bodyCtrl = TextEditingController();
  bool _isPinned = false;
  bool _isLoading = false;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _titleCtrl.dispose();
    _bodyCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _submit() async {
    if (_titleCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty || _bodyCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty) {
      setState(() => _error = 'Заполните заголовок и текст');
      return;
    }
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      await AnnouncementsRepository.instance.create(
        buildingId: widget.buildingId,
        title: _titleCtrl.text.trim(),
        body: _bodyCtrl.text.trim(),
        isPinned: _isPinned,
      );
      if (mounted) Navigator.pop(context, true);
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      setState(() => _error = e.message);
    } catch (_) {
      setState(() => _error = 'Не удалось опубликовать');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    return Padding(
      padding: EdgeInsets.only(
        left: AppDimens.spaceLg,
        right: AppDimens.spaceLg,
        top: AppDimens.spaceLg,
        bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom + AppDimens.spaceLg,
      ),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text('Новое объявление',
              style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: AppTypography.bold)),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
          TextField(
            controller: _titleCtrl,
            style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary),
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              hintText: 'Заголовок',
              hintStyle: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary),
              filled: true,
              fillColor: c.surfaceVariant,
              border: OutlineInputBorder(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd), borderSide: BorderSide.none),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
          TextField(
            controller: _bodyCtrl,
            maxLines: 4,
            style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary),
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              hintText: 'Текст объявления',
              hintStyle: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary),
              filled: true,
              fillColor: c.surfaceVariant,
              border: OutlineInputBorder(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd), borderSide: BorderSide.none),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
          SwitchListTile(
            contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
            value: _isPinned,
            onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _isPinned = v),
            activeThumbColor: c.primary,
            title: Text('Закрепить', style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary)),
          ),
          if (_error != null) ...[
            Text(_error!, style: TextStyle(color: c.danger)),
            const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
          ],
          SizedBox(
            width: double.infinity,
            height: AppDimens.primaryTapTarget,
            child: ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _submit,
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                backgroundColor: c.primary,
                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd)),
              ),
              child: _isLoading
                  ? const SizedBox(
                      width: 22, height: 22,
                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2.5))
                  : const Text('Опубликовать',
                      style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: AppTypography.bold)),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:lucide_icons/lucide_icons.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/auth_repository.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/osi_repository.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/events_repository.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/screens/login_page.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/screens/chairman_requests_screen.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/screens/chairman_announcements_screen.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/screens/apartments_residents_screen.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/screens/notifications_screen.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/screens/feature_catalog_list.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/screens/chairman_polls_screen.dart';

// Оболочка председателя (REQ-143, ревизия REQ-121 — срез R2; таб «Голоса» — срез 23):
// оперативные функции в приложении (заявки дома, объявления, голосования, уведомления);
// управление (финансы, отчёты, импорт) — на сайте (REQ-145). Таб-бар: Главная / Заявки /
// Объявления / Голоса / Ещё.
class ChairmanHomeShell extends StatefulWidget {
  final AuthUser user;
  const ChairmanHomeShell({super.key, required this.user});

  @override
  State<ChairmanHomeShell> createState() => _ChairmanHomeShellState();
}

class _ChairmanHomeShellState extends State<ChairmanHomeShell> {
  int _tab = 0;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    // Живые обновления (срез 24) — один коннект на всё время сессии председателя в приложении.
    EventsRepository.instance.connect();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    EventsRepository.instance.disconnect();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    final pages = [
      _ChairmanHomeTab(user: widget.user),
      const ChairmanRequestsScreen(),
      const ChairmanAnnouncementsScreen(),
      const ChairmanPollsScreen(),
      _ChairmanMoreTab(user: widget.user),
    ];

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: c.background,
      body: IndexedStack(index: _tab, children: pages),
      bottomNavigationBar: NavigationBarTheme(
        data: NavigationBarThemeData(
          backgroundColor: c.card,
          indicatorColor: c.primary.withOpacity(0.15),
          labelTextStyle: WidgetStateProperty.resolveWith((states) {
            final selected = states.contains(WidgetState.selected);
            return TextStyle(
              fontSize: AppTypography.caption,
              color: selected ? c.primary : c.textTertiary,
              fontWeight: selected ? AppTypography.semibold : AppTypography.regular,
            );
          }),
          iconTheme: WidgetStateProperty.resolveWith((states) {
            final selected = states.contains(WidgetState.selected);
            return IconThemeData(color: selected ? c.primary : c.textTertiary);
          }),
        ),
        child: NavigationBar(
          selectedIndex: _tab,
          onDestinationSelected: (i) => setState(() => _tab = i),
          destinations: const [
            NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(LucideIcons.layoutDashboard), label: 'Главная'),
            NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(LucideIcons.clipboardList), label: 'Заявки'),
            NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(LucideIcons.megaphone), label: 'Объявления'),
            NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(LucideIcons.vote), label: 'Голоса'),
            NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(LucideIcons.moreHorizontal), label: 'Ещё'),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ChairmanHomeTab extends StatefulWidget {
  final AuthUser user;
  const _ChairmanHomeTab({required this.user});

  @override
  State<_ChairmanHomeTab> createState() => _ChairmanHomeTabState();
}

class _ChairmanHomeTabState extends State<_ChairmanHomeTab> {
  List<Building> _buildings = [];
  List<PendingJoinRequest> _joinRequests = [];
  bool _loading = true;
  final Set<String> _resolvingIds = {};

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
  }

  // Дома и заявки на подключение грузятся вместе (review 2026-07-22: раньше панель заявок
  // читала себя один раз в своём initState и не обновлялась вместе с pull-to-refresh родителя —
  // после привязки жителя список оставался неактуальным до перезахода в приложение).
  Future<void> _load() async {
    try {
      final results = await Future.wait([
        OsiRepository.instance.listBuildings(),
        OsiRepository.instance.listOsiJoinRequests(),
      ]);
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _buildings = results[0] as List<Building>;
          _joinRequests = results[1] as List<PendingJoinRequest>;
          _loading = false;
        });
      }
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _loading = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _resolveJoinRequest(String id) async {
    setState(() => _resolvingIds.add(id));
    try {
      await OsiRepository.instance.resolveJoinRequest(id);
      if (mounted) setState(() => _joinRequests.removeWhere((r) => r.id == id));
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _resolvingIds.remove(id));
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: c.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: c.background,
        elevation: 0,
        title: const Text('Fixly'),
        titleTextStyle: TextStyle(
            color: c.textPrimary, fontSize: AppTypography.screenTitle, fontWeight: AppTypography.bold),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: Icon(LucideIcons.bell, color: c.textPrimary),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.push(
              context,
              MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const NotificationsScreen()),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: _load,
        child: ListView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
          children: [
            if (widget.user.fullName != null)
              Text('Здравствуйте, ${widget.user.fullName}!',
                  style: TextStyle(
                      color: c.textPrimary, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: AppTypography.bold)),
            const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceLg),
            _JoinRequestsSection(
              requests: _joinRequests,
              resolvingIds: _resolvingIds,
              onResolve: _resolveJoinRequest,
            ),
            Text('Ваши дома',
                style: TextStyle(
                    color: c.textPrimary,
                    fontSize: AppTypography.sectionTitle,
                    fontWeight: AppTypography.bold)),
            const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
            if (_loading)
              const Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
                child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
              )
            else if (_buildings.isEmpty)
              _EmptyBuildingsCard(colors: c)
            else
              for (final b in _buildings)
                Container(
                  margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppDimens.spaceSm),
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: c.card,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
                    border: Border.all(color: c.cardBorder),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      Icon(LucideIcons.building2, color: c.primary),
                      const SizedBox(width: AppDimens.spaceSm),
                      Expanded(child: Text(b.address, style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary))),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
            const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
            OutlinedButton.icon(
              onPressed: () async {
                await Navigator.push(
                  context,
                  MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const ApartmentsResidentsScreen()),
                );
                if (mounted) _load();
              },
              icon: Icon(LucideIcons.users, color: c.primary),
              label: Text('Дома и жители', style: TextStyle(color: c.primary)),
              style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                minimumSize: const Size(double.infinity, AppDimens.minTapTarget),
                side: BorderSide(color: c.primary),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
            Text(
              'Полное управление (финансы, отчёты, импорт квартир) — на сайте.',
              style: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary, fontSize: AppTypography.caption),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// Заявки на подключение (REQ-152/153, docs/specs/osi-join-request.md — портировано с веба,
// JoinRequestsPanel.tsx): контакт заявителя + пометка, привязку к квартире председатель делает
// как раньше (ApartmentsResidentsScreen → «Житель» → поиск по телефону/email), «Обработано»
// только снимает заявку со списка.
// Stateless (review 2026-07-22) — данные и обработчик приходят от _ChairmanHomeTabState, чтобы
// pull-to-refresh родителя обновлял и список заявок, а не только дома.
class _JoinRequestsSection extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<PendingJoinRequest> requests;
  final Set<String> resolvingIds;
  final ValueChanged<String> onResolve;

  const _JoinRequestsSection({
    required this.requests,
    required this.resolvingIds,
    required this.onResolve,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (requests.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Row(
          children: [
            Icon(LucideIcons.userCheck, color: c.primary, size: 18),
            const SizedBox(width: AppDimens.spaceXs),
            Text('Заявки на подключение (${requests.length})',
                style: TextStyle(
                    color: c.textPrimary,
                    fontSize: AppTypography.sectionTitle,
                    fontWeight: AppTypography.bold)),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
        for (final r in requests)
          AppCard(
            margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppDimens.spaceSm),
            child: Row(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(r.fullName ?? (r.email ?? r.phone ?? '—'),
                          style: TextStyle(
                              color: c.textPrimary, fontWeight: AppTypography.semibold)),
                      if (r.fullName != null && (r.email != null || r.phone != null))
                        Text(r.email ?? r.phone ?? '',
                            style:
                                TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary, fontSize: AppTypography.caption)),
                      if (r.note != null) ...[
                        const SizedBox(height: 4),
                        Text(r.note!,
                            style: TextStyle(color: c.textSecondary, fontSize: AppTypography.body)),
                      ],
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: AppDimens.spaceSm),
                OutlinedButton(
                  onPressed: resolvingIds.contains(r.id) ? null : () => onResolve(r.id),
                  child: Text(resolvingIds.contains(r.id) ? '…' : 'Обработано'),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceLg),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _EmptyBuildingsCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final AppColors colors;
  const _EmptyBuildingsCard({required this.colors});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = colors;

      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Icon(LucideIcons.building2, color: c.textTertiary),
          const SizedBox(width: AppDimens.spaceSm),
          Expanded(
            child: Text('Домов пока нет — добавьте в разделе «Дома и жители»',
                style: TextStyle(color: c.textSecondary)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ChairmanMoreTab extends StatelessWidget {
  final AuthUser user;
  const _ChairmanMoreTab({required this.user});

  Future<void> _logout(BuildContext context) async {
    await AuthRepository.instance.logout();
    if (!context.mounted) return;
    Navigator.pushAndRemoveUntil(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const LoginPage()),
      (_) => false,
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: c.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: c.background,
        elevation: 0,
        title: const Text('Ещё'),
        titleTextStyle: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary, fontSize: AppTypography.screenTitle),
      ),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: c.card,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
              border: Border.all(color: c.cardBorder),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                if (user.email != null)
                  _row(c, LucideIcons.mail, user.email!),
                if (user.phone != null)
                  _row(c, LucideIcons.phone, user.phone!),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
          _menuTile(context, c, LucideIcons.users, 'Дома и жители',
              () => Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const ApartmentsResidentsScreen()))),
          _menuTile(context, c, LucideIcons.bell, 'Уведомления',
              () => Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const NotificationsScreen()))),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceLg),
          const FeatureCatalogSection(isChairman: true),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
          SizedBox(
            height: AppDimens.primaryTapTarget,
            child: OutlinedButton.icon(
              onPressed: () => _logout(context),
              icon: Icon(LucideIcons.logOut, color: c.danger),
              label: Text('Выйти', style: TextStyle(color: c.danger)),
              style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(side: BorderSide(color: c.danger.withOpacity(0.4))),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _row(AppColors c, IconData icon, String label) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 6),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Icon(icon, size: 18, color: c.textTertiary),
          const SizedBox(width: AppDimens.spaceSm),
          Text(label, style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _menuTile(BuildContext context, AppColors c, IconData icon, String label, VoidCallback onTap) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: onTap,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
      child: Container(
        margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppDimens.spaceSm),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: c.card,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
          border: Border.all(color: c.cardBorder),
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Icon(icon, color: c.primary, size: 20),
            const SizedBox(width: AppDimens.spaceSm),
            Expanded(child: Text(label, style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary))),
            Icon(Icons.chevron_right, color: c.textTertiary, size: 18),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/api_client.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/osi_repository.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/polls_repository.dart';

// Опросы дома — председатель (срез 23, REQ-143). Список с результатами (без голосования —
// председатель всегда видит проценты, как showResults на сайте) + создание. MVP: берём ПЕРВЫЙ
// дом ОСИ (тот же паттерн, что chairman_announcements_screen.dart из R2 — выбор дома при
// нескольких вне объёма, отмечено в build-plan-3). Deadline в форме не выставляется — как и
// на сайте (PollForm.tsx), хотя API его принимает.
class ChairmanPollsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const ChairmanPollsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<ChairmanPollsScreen> createState() => _ChairmanPollsScreenState();
}

class _ChairmanPollsScreenState extends State<ChairmanPollsScreen> {
  List<Poll> _polls = [];
  String? _buildingId;
  bool _loading = true;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    setState(() {
      _loading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final buildings = await OsiRepository.instance.listBuildings();
      final buildingId = buildings.isNotEmpty ? buildings.first.id : null;
      final polls = buildingId != null
          ? await PollsRepository.instance.list(buildingId: buildingId)
          : <Poll>[];
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _buildingId = buildingId;
        _polls = polls;
        _loading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _error = e.toString();
        _loading = false;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _openCreate() async {
    if (_buildingId == null) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
          .showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('Сначала добавьте дом')));
      return;
    }
    final created = await showModalBottomSheet<bool>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.of(context).card,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(AppDimens.radiusLg)),
      ),
      builder: (_) => _CreatePollSheet(buildingId: _buildingId!),
    );
    if (created == true && mounted) _load();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: c.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: c.background,
        elevation: 0,
        title: Text('Голосования', style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary)),
        iconTheme: IconThemeData(color: c.textPrimary),
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton(
        onPressed: _openCreate,
        backgroundColor: c.primary,
        child: const Icon(Icons.add, color: Colors.white),
      ),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: _load,
        child: _loading
            ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
            : _error != null
                ? Center(
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceLg),
                      child: Column(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          Text(_error!, style: TextStyle(color: c.danger), textAlign: TextAlign.center),
                          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
                          OutlinedButton(onPressed: _load, child: const Text('Повторить')),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  )
                : _polls.isEmpty
                    ? Center(
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceLg),
                          child: Text('Опросов пока нет — нажмите «+», чтобы создать первый',
                              textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary)),
                        ),
                      )
                    : ListView(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                            AppDimens.spaceMd, AppDimens.spaceMd, AppDimens.spaceMd, 80),
                        children: [
                          for (final poll in _polls)
                            Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppDimens.spaceSm),
                              child: _ResultCard(poll: poll, colors: c),
                            ),
                        ],
                      ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ResultCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final Poll poll;
  final AppColors colors;
  const _ResultCard({required this.poll, required this.colors});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = colors;
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: c.card,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
        border: Border.all(color: c.cardBorder),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(poll.question,
                    style: TextStyle(
                        color: c.textPrimary,
                        fontSize: AppTypography.cardTitle,
                        fontWeight: AppTypography.bold)),
              ),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: (poll.closed ? c.textTertiary : c.success).withOpacity(0.12),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  poll.closed ? 'Завершён' : 'Активен',
                  style: TextStyle(
                      color: poll.closed ? c.textTertiary : c.success,
                      fontSize: 11,
                      fontWeight: AppTypography.semibold),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 2),
          Text('Голосов: ${poll.totalVotes}',
              style: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary, fontSize: AppTypography.caption)),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
          for (final option in poll.options)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
              child: _bar(c, option, poll.totalVotes),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _bar(AppColors c, PollOption option, int totalVotes) {
    final pct = totalVotes == 0 ? 0 : (option.votes / totalVotes * 100).round();
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(child: Text(option.label, style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary))),
            Text('$pct% (${option.votes})',
                style: TextStyle(color: c.textSecondary, fontSize: AppTypography.caption)),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        ClipRRect(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
          child: LayoutBuilder(
            builder: (context, constraints) => Stack(
              children: [
                Container(height: 8, color: c.surfaceVariant),
                Container(height: 8, width: constraints.maxWidth * (pct / 100), color: c.primary),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _CreatePollSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  final String buildingId;
  const _CreatePollSheet({required this.buildingId});

  @override
  State<_CreatePollSheet> createState() => _CreatePollSheetState();
}

class _CreatePollSheetState extends State<_CreatePollSheet> {
  final _questionCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final List<TextEditingController> _optionCtrls = [
    TextEditingController(text: 'Да'),
    TextEditingController(text: 'Нет'),
  ];
  bool _isLoading = false;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _questionCtrl.dispose();
    for (final ctrl in _optionCtrls) {
      ctrl.dispose();
    }
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _addOption() {
    if (_optionCtrls.length >= 6) return;
    setState(() => _optionCtrls.add(TextEditingController()));
  }

  void _removeOption(int index) {
    if (_optionCtrls.length <= 2) return;
    setState(() => _optionCtrls.removeAt(index).dispose());
  }

  Future<void> _submit() async {
    final question = _questionCtrl.text.trim();
    final options = _optionCtrls.map((c) => c.text.trim()).where((s) => s.isNotEmpty).toList();
    if (question.length < 3) {
      setState(() => _error = 'Вопрос — минимум 3 символа');
      return;
    }
    if (options.length < 2) {
      setState(() => _error = 'Нужно минимум 2 варианта ответа');
      return;
    }
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      await PollsRepository.instance.create(
        buildingId: widget.buildingId,
        question: question,
        options: options,
      );
      if (mounted) Navigator.pop(context, true);
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      setState(() => _error = e.message);
    } catch (_) {
      setState(() => _error = 'Не удалось создать опрос');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    return Padding(
      padding: EdgeInsets.only(
        left: AppDimens.spaceLg,
        right: AppDimens.spaceLg,
        top: AppDimens.spaceLg,
        bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom + AppDimens.spaceLg,
      ),
      child: SingleChildScrollView(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text('Новый опрос',
                style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: AppTypography.bold)),
            const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
            TextField(
              controller: _questionCtrl,
              style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary),
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                hintText: 'Вопрос',
                hintStyle: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary),
                filled: true,
                fillColor: c.surfaceVariant,
                border: OutlineInputBorder(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd), borderSide: BorderSide.none),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
            for (var i = 0; i < _optionCtrls.length; i++)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      child: TextField(
                        controller: _optionCtrls[i],
                        style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary),
                        decoration: InputDecoration(
                          hintText: 'Вариант ${i + 1}',
                          hintStyle: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary),
                          filled: true,
                          fillColor: c.surfaceVariant,
                          border: OutlineInputBorder(
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
                              borderSide: BorderSide.none),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    if (_optionCtrls.length > 2)
                      IconButton(
                        onPressed: () => _removeOption(i),
                        icon: Icon(Icons.close, color: c.textTertiary, size: 18),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            if (_optionCtrls.length < 6)
              TextButton.icon(
                onPressed: _addOption,
                icon: Icon(Icons.add, color: c.primary, size: 18),
                label: Text('Вариант', style: TextStyle(color: c.primary)),
                style: TextButton.styleFrom(padding: EdgeInsets.zero, alignment: Alignment.centerLeft),
              ),
            if (_error != null) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
              Text(_error!, style: TextStyle(color: c.danger)),
            ],
            const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
            SizedBox(
              width: double.infinity,
              height: AppDimens.primaryTapTarget,
              child: ElevatedButton(
                onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _submit,
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: c.primary,
                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd)),
                ),
                child: _isLoading
                    ? const SizedBox(
                        width: 22, height: 22,
                        child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2.5))
                    : const Text('Создать опрос',
                        style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: AppTypography.bold)),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

=== ФАЙЛ: lib\screens\chairman_requests_screen.dart ===
=========================================

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/requests_repository.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/request_labels.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/events_repository.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/screens/request_thread_screen.dart';

// Заявки дома (REQ-143/144, срез R2) — все заявки ОСИ (GET ?scope=building), председатель
// меняет статус прямо из списка (тред открывается тем же RequestThreadScreen, что у жителя —
// сервер уже разрешает читать/писать в тред и жителю-автору, и председателю, REQ-003).
// Живые обновления вместо поллинга — срез 24, REQ-133.
class ChairmanRequestsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const ChairmanRequestsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<ChairmanRequestsScreen> createState() => _ChairmanRequestsScreenState();
}

const List<String> _statusOrder = ['RECEIVED', 'IN_PROGRESS', 'RESOLVED'];

class _ChairmanRequestsScreenState extends State<ChairmanRequestsScreen> {
  List<ServiceRequest> _items = [];
  bool _loading = true;
  String? _error;
  StreamSubscription<SseEvent>? _sub;
  Timer? _debounce;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
    _sub = EventsRepository.instance.stream.listen((e) {
      if (e.entityType == 'request') {
        _debounce?.cancel();
        _debounce = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 500), _load);
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    _sub?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    setState(() {
      _loading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final items = await RequestsRepository.instance.listBuilding();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _items = items;
        _loading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _error = e.toString();
        _loading = false;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _advanceStatus(ServiceRequest r) async {
    final idx = _statusOrder.indexOf(r.status);
    if (idx < 0 || idx >= _statusOrder.length - 1) return; // уже RESOLVED
    try {
      await RequestsRepository.instance.setStatus(r.id, _statusOrder[idx + 1]);
      if (mounted) _load();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString())));
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: c.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: c.background,
        elevation: 0,
        title: Text('Заявки дома', style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary)),
        iconTheme: IconThemeData(color: c.textPrimary),
      ),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: _load,
        child: _loading
            ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
            : _error != null
                ? Center(
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceLg),
                      child: Column(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          Text(_error!, style: TextStyle(color: c.danger), textAlign: TextAlign.center),
                          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
                          OutlinedButton(onPressed: _load, child: const Text('Повторить')),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  )
                : _items.isEmpty
                    ? Center(
                        child: Text('Заявок пока нет',
                            style: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary, fontSize: AppTypography.body)),
                      )
                    : ListView.separated(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
                        itemCount: _items.length,
                        separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
                        itemBuilder: (_, i) {
                          final r = _items[i];
                          final color = r.status == 'RESOLVED'
                              ? c.success
                              : r.status == 'IN_PROGRESS'
                                  ? c.warning
                                  : c.textTertiary;
                          final canAdvance = _statusOrder.indexOf(r.status) < _statusOrder.length - 1;
                          return Container(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: c.card,
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
                              border: Border.all(color: c.cardBorder),
                            ),
                            child: Column(
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                              children: [
                                InkWell(
                                  onTap: () async {
                                    await Navigator.push(
                                      context,
                                      MaterialPageRoute(
                                        builder: (_) => RequestThreadScreen(requestId: r.id),
                                      ),
                                    );
                                    if (mounted) _load();
                                  },
                                  child: Row(
                                    children: [
                                      Expanded(
                                        child: Column(
                                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                          children: [
                                            Text(requestCategoryLabels[r.category] ?? r.category,
                                                style: TextStyle(
                                                    color: c.textPrimary,
                                                    fontWeight: AppTypography.semibold)),
                                            const SizedBox(height: 4),
                                            Text(r.text,
                                                maxLines: 2,
                                                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                                style: TextStyle(color: c.textSecondary)),
                                            const SizedBox(height: 4),
                                            Text(
                                              DateFormat('d MMMM', 'ru').format(r.createdAt.toLocal()),
                                              style: TextStyle(
                                                  color: c.textTertiary, fontSize: AppTypography.caption),
                                            ),
                                          ],
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                      Container(
                                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 5),
                                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                          color: color.withOpacity(0.12),
                                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
                                        ),
                                        child: Text(
                                          requestStatusLabels[r.status] ?? r.status,
                                          style: TextStyle(
                                              color: color,
                                              fontSize: AppTypography.caption,
                                              fontWeight: AppTypography.semibold),
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  ),
                                ),
                                if (canAdvance) ...[
                                  const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
                                  Align(
                                    alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                                    child: OutlinedButton(
                                      onPressed: () => _advanceStatus(r),
                                      style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(side: BorderSide(color: c.primary)),
                                      child: Text(
                                        r.status == 'RECEIVED' ? 'Взять в работу' : 'Отметить решённой',
                                        style: TextStyle(color: c.primary),
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ],
                            ),
                          );
                        },
                      ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

=== ФАЙЛ: lib\screens\create_osi_screen.dart ===
