import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/auth_repository.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/master_repository.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/screens/login_page.dart';

// Статус анкеты мастера вне APPROVED (REQ-148, срез 25) — тот же смысл, что «на проверке» в
// master_application_screen.dart (сразу после отправки формы), но теперь достижим ПОВТОРНО
// после перезапуска приложения: там это было состояние формы в памяти, здесь — перечитывается
// через GET /api/master/me при каждом заходе HomeRouter'а.
class MasterStatusScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  final MasterProfile master;
  const MasterStatusScreen({super.key, required this.master});

  Future<void> _logout(BuildContext context) async {
    await AuthRepository.instance.logout();
    if (!context.mounted) return;
    Navigator.pushAndRemoveUntil(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const LoginPage()),
      (_) => false,
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    final (icon, iconColor, title, message) = switch (master.status) {
      'REJECTED' => (
          Icons.report_gmailerrorred,
          c.danger,
          'Анкета отклонена',
          'Свяжитесь с поддержкой, чтобы уточнить причину.',
        ),
      'BLOCKED' => (
          Icons.block,
          c.danger,
          'Аккаунт заблокирован',
          'Свяжитесь с поддержкой.',
        ),
      _ => (
          Icons.access_time,
          c.warning,
          'Анкета на проверке',
          'Мы проверим анкету и откроем доступ к заказам вашего города. '
              'Обычно это занимает немного времени.',
        ),
    };

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: c.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: c.background,
        elevation: 0,
        automaticallyImplyLeading: false,
        title: const Text('Мастер'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.logout, color: c.textPrimary), onPressed: () => _logout(context)),
        ],
      ),
      body: Center(
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceLg),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              Icon(icon, size: 48, color: iconColor),
              const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
              Text(title,
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  style:
                      TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: AppTypography.bold)),
              const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
              Text('${master.fullName} · ${master.city}',
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary)),
              const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
              Text(message, textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: TextStyle(color: c.textSecondary)),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


=========================================
=== ФАЙЛ: lib\screens\notifications_screen.dart ===
=========================================

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/notifications_repository.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/events_repository.dart';

// Центр уведомлений жителя (REQ-100, срез 22). Только чтение + отметка прочитанным —
// уведомления создаёт сервер по триггерам (объявление/заявка/сообщение). Каждое SSE-событие
// (срез 24) соответствует ровно одной новой Notification — слушаем весь поток без фильтра
// по entityType, в отличие от списков заявок/объявлений.
class NotificationsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const NotificationsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<NotificationsScreen> createState() => _NotificationsScreenState();
}

class _NotificationsScreenState extends State<NotificationsScreen> {
  List<AppNotification> _items = [];
  bool _loading = true;
  String? _error;
  StreamSubscription<SseEvent>? _sub;
  Timer? _debounce;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
    _sub = EventsRepository.instance.stream.listen((_) {
      _debounce?.cancel();
      _debounce = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 500), _load);
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    _sub?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    setState(() {
      _loading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final page = await NotificationsRepository.instance.list();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _items = page.notifications;
        _loading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _error = e.toString();
        _loading = false;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _markAllRead() async {
    await NotificationsRepository.instance.markRead();
    if (mounted) _load();
  }

  Future<void> _onTapItem(AppNotification n) async {
    if (!n.isRead) {
      await NotificationsRepository.instance.markRead(id: n.id);
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          final idx = _items.indexWhere((x) => x.id == n.id);
          if (idx != -1) {
            _items[idx] = AppNotification(
              id: n.id,
              type: n.type,
              title: n.title,
              body: n.body,
              entityType: n.entityType,
              entityId: n.entityId,
              readAt: DateTime.now(),
              createdAt: n.createdAt,
            );
          }
        });
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    final hasUnread = _items.any((n) => !n.isRead);

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: c.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: c.background,
        elevation: 0,
        title: Text('Уведомления', style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary)),
        iconTheme: IconThemeData(color: c.textPrimary),
        actions: [
          if (hasUnread)
            TextButton(
              onPressed: _markAllRead,
              child: Text('Прочитать всё', style: TextStyle(color: c.primary)),
            ),
        ],
      ),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: _load,
        child: _loading
            ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
            : _error != null
                ? _ErrorState(message: _error!, onRetry: _load)
                : _items.isEmpty
                    ? _EmptyState(color: c)
                    : ListView.separated(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
                        itemCount: _items.length,
                        separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
                        itemBuilder: (_, i) => _NotificationTile(
                          notification: _items[i],
                          onTap: () => _onTapItem(_items[i]),
                        ),
                      ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _NotificationTile extends StatelessWidget {
  final AppNotification notification;
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  const _NotificationTile({required this.notification, required this.onTap});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    final n = notification;
    return InkWell(
      onTap: onTap,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: n.isRead ? c.card : c.primary.withOpacity(0.06),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
          border: Border.all(color: c.cardBorder),
        ),
        child: Row(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            if (!n.isRead)
              Container(
                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 6, right: AppDimens.spaceSm),
                width: 8,
                height: 8,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: c.primary, shape: BoxShape.circle),
              )
            else
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    n.title,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: c.textPrimary,
                      fontSize: AppTypography.cardTitle,
                      fontWeight: n.isRead ? AppTypography.regular : AppTypography.bold,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text(n.body,
                      style: TextStyle(color: c.textSecondary, fontSize: AppTypography.body)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 6),
                  Text(
                    DateFormat('d MMMM, HH:mm', 'ru').format(n.createdAt.toLocal()),
                    style: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary, fontSize: AppTypography.caption),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _EmptyState extends StatelessWidget {
  final AppColors color;
  const _EmptyState({required this.color});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Center(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceLg),
        child: Text(
          'Пока нет уведомлений',
          style: TextStyle(color: color.textTertiary, fontSize: AppTypography.body),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ErrorState extends StatelessWidget {
  final String message;
  final VoidCallback onRetry;
  const _ErrorState({required this.message, required this.onRetry});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    return Center(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceLg),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Text(message, style: TextStyle(color: c.danger), textAlign: TextAlign.center),
            const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
            OutlinedButton(onPressed: onRetry, child: const Text('Повторить')),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


=========================================
=== ФАЙЛ: lib\screens\polls_screen.dart ===
=========================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:lucide_icons/lucide_icons.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/polls_repository.dart';

// Опросы дома, вид жителя (срез 23) — вкладки Активные/Завершённые, как на сайте
// (fixlypwa /app/polls). До голоса — варианты как кнопки; после голоса/для закрытых —
// прогресс-бары с процентами (те же формулы, что PollCard.tsx на сайте).
class PollsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const PollsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<PollsScreen> createState() => _PollsScreenState();
}

class _PollsScreenState extends State<PollsScreen>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late final TabController _tabController;
  List<Poll> _polls = [];
  bool _loading = true;
  String? _error;
  String? _votingOptionId; // блокирует повторный тап, пока идёт запрос

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _tabController = TabController(length: 2, vsync: this);
    _load();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _tabController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    setState(() {
      _loading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final polls = await PollsRepository.instance.list();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _polls = polls;
        _loading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _error = e.toString();
        _loading = false;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _vote(String optionId) async {
    setState(() => _votingOptionId = optionId);
    try {
      await PollsRepository.instance.vote(optionId);
      await _load();
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString())));
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _votingOptionId = null);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    final active = _polls.where((p) => !p.closed).toList();
    final closed = _polls.where((p) => p.closed).toList();
    final waiting = active.where((p) => !p.voted).length;

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: c.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: c.background,
        elevation: 0,
        title: Text('Голосования', style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary)),
        iconTheme: IconThemeData(color: c.textPrimary),
        bottom: TabBar(
          controller: _tabController,
          labelColor: c.primary,
          unselectedLabelColor: c.textTertiary,
          indicatorColor: c.primary,
          tabs: [
            Tab(text: waiting > 0 ? 'Активные ($waiting)' : 'Активные'),
            const Tab(text: 'Завершённые'),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      body: _loading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : _error != null
              ? Center(
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceLg),
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Text(_error!, style: TextStyle(color: c.danger), textAlign: TextAlign.center),
                        const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
                        OutlinedButton(onPressed: _load, child: const Text('Повторить')),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                )
              : TabBarView(
                  controller: _tabController,
                  children: [
                    _PollList(polls: active, colors: c, onRefresh: _load, onVote: _vote, votingOptionId: _votingOptionId),
                    _PollList(polls: closed, colors: c, onRefresh: _load, onVote: _vote, votingOptionId: _votingOptionId),
                  ],
                ),
    );
  }
}

class _PollList extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<Poll> polls;
  final AppColors colors;
  final Future<void> Function() onRefresh;
  final Future<void> Function(String optionId) onVote;
  final String? votingOptionId;

  const _PollList({
    required this.polls,
    required this.colors,
    required this.onRefresh,
    required this.onVote,
    required this.votingOptionId,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = colors;
    return RefreshIndicator(
      onRefresh: onRefresh,
      child: polls.isEmpty
          ? ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceLg),
              children: [
                Center(
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 60),
                    child: Text('Пока пусто', style: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary)),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            )
          : ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
              children: [
                for (final poll in polls)
                  Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppDimens.spaceSm),
                    child: _PollCard(poll: poll, colors: c, onVote: onVote, votingOptionId: votingOptionId),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
    );
  }
}

class _PollCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final Poll poll;
  final AppColors colors;
  final Future<void> Function(String optionId) onVote;
  final String? votingOptionId;

  const _PollCard({
    required this.poll,
    required this.colors,
    required this.onVote,
    required this.votingOptionId,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = colors;
    final showResults = poll.voted || poll.closed;

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: c.card,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
        border: Border.all(color: c.cardBorder),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(poll.question,
                    style: TextStyle(
                        color: c.textPrimary,
                        fontSize: AppTypography.cardTitle,
                        fontWeight: AppTypography.bold)),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: AppDimens.spaceSm),
              _statusChip(c),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
          for (final option in poll.options)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
              child: showResults
                  ? _ResultRow(option: option, totalVotes: poll.totalVotes, colors: c, mine: option.id == poll.myOptionId)
                  : _OptionButton(
                      option: option,
                      colors: c,
                      busy: votingOptionId == option.id,
                      onTap: votingOptionId == null ? () => onVote(option.id) : null,
                    ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _statusChip(AppColors c) {
    final (text, color) = poll.closed
        ? ('Завершён', c.textTertiary)
        : poll.voted
            ? ('Вы проголосовали', c.success)
            : ('Нужен ваш голос', c.primary);
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withOpacity(0.12),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
      ),
      child: Text(text, style: TextStyle(color: color, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: AppTypography.semibold)),
    );
  }
}

class _OptionButton extends StatelessWidget {
  final PollOption option;
  final AppColors colors;
  final bool busy;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;

  const _OptionButton({required this.option, required this.colors, required this.busy, this.onTap});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = colors;
    return InkWell(
      onTap: onTap,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
      child: Container(
        width: double.infinity,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: AppDimens.spaceMd, vertical: 12),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          border: Border.all(color: c.cardBorder),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(child: Text(option.label, style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary))),
            if (busy)
              SizedBox(
                width: 16,
                height: 16,
                child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2, color: c.primary),
              )
            else
              Icon(LucideIcons.circle, size: 18, color: c.textTertiary),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ResultRow extends StatelessWidget {
  final PollOption option;
  final int totalVotes;
  final AppColors colors;
  final bool mine;

  const _ResultRow({required this.option, required this.totalVotes, required this.colors, required this.mine});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = colors;
    final pct = totalVotes == 0 ? 0 : (option.votes / totalVotes * 100).round();
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                option.label,
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: c.textPrimary,
                  fontWeight: mine ? AppTypography.bold : AppTypography.regular,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            Text('$pct% (${option.votes})',
                style: TextStyle(color: c.textSecondary, fontSize: AppTypography.caption)),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        ClipRRect(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
          child: LayoutBuilder(
            builder: (context, constraints) => Stack(
              children: [
                Container(height: 8, color: c.surfaceVariant),
                Container(
                  height: 8,
                  width: constraints.maxWidth * (pct / 100),
                  color: mine ? c.primary : c.textTertiary,
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}


=========================================
=== ФАЙЛ: lib\screens\register_screen.dart ===
=========================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:lucide_icons/lucide_icons.dart';
import 'package:flutter_dotenv/flutter_dotenv.dart';
import 'package:url_launcher/url_launcher.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/api_client.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/auth_repository.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/screens/home_router.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/screens/create_osi_screen.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/screens/master_application_screen.dart';

// Регистрация с выбором роли (onboarding-unified.md §0, срез R2 — запрос Рахима 2026-07-17:
// «Регистрация с выбором роли: председатель, житель или мастер»). Технически — тот же
// единственный аккаунт, что и «Вход» (auth-email.md §3, первый код сам создаёт пользователя):
// разница только в собираемых полях (роль + ФИО) и в шаге ПОСЛЕ кода — куда ведём дальше
// определяет выбранная роль, не хранимое серверное состояние (сервер роль не назначает по
// этому выбору — она возникает из действия: создал ОСИ / привязан председателем / анкета
// одобрена, REQ-137/138).
class RegisterScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const RegisterScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<RegisterScreen> createState() => _RegisterScreenState();
}

enum _Step { role, form, code }

class _Role {
  final String value;
  final String label;
  final IconData icon;
  const _Role(this.value, this.label, this.icon);
}

const _roles = [
  _Role('RESIDENT', 'Житель', LucideIcons.home),
  _Role('CHAIRMAN', 'Председатель', LucideIcons.building2),
  _Role('MASTER', 'Мастер', LucideIcons.wrench),
];

class _RegisterScreenState extends State<RegisterScreen> {
  _Step _step = _Step.role;
  String? _selectedRole;

  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _fullNameCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _identifierCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _codeCtrl = TextEditingController();

  bool _isLoading = false;
  String? _error;
  String _identifier = '';
  String _fullName = '';
  String? _devCode;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _fullNameCtrl.dispose();
    _identifierCtrl.dispose();
    _codeCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _pickRole(String role) {
    setState(() {
      _selectedRole = role;
      _step = _Step.form;
      _error = null;
    });
  }

  Future<void> _requestCode() async {
    if (!(_formKey.currentState?.validate() ?? false)) return;
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      _fullName = _fullNameCtrl.text.trim();
      _identifier = _identifierCtrl.text.trim();
      final devCode = await AuthRepository.instance.requestOtp(_identifier);
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _step = _Step.code;
          _devCode = devCode;
        });
      }
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      setState(() => _error = e.message);
    } catch (_) {
      setState(() => _error = 'Ошибка подключения');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _verifyAndRoute() async {
    final code = _codeCtrl.text.trim();
    if (code.length != 6) {
      setState(() => _error = 'Код — 6 цифр');
      return;
    }
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final user = await AuthRepository.instance.verifyOtp(_identifier, code, fullName: _fullName);
      if (!mounted) return;

      // Куда ведём ПОСЛЕ кода — по роли, выбранной на этом экране (REQ-137). Роли пользователя
      // на сервере (user.roles) в этот момент всё ещё пустые — они появятся из следующего
      // действия (создание ОСИ / привязка председателем / одобрение анкеты).
      switch (_selectedRole) {
        case 'CHAIRMAN':
          Navigator.pushAndRemoveUntil(
            context,
            MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const CreateOsiScreen()),
            (_) => false,
          );
          break;
        case 'MASTER':
          Navigator.pushAndRemoveUntil(
            context,
            MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => MasterApplicationScreen(prefillFullName: _fullName)),
            (_) => false,
          );
          break;
        default: // RESIDENT
          Navigator.pushAndRemoveUntil(
            context,
            MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => HomeRouter(user: user)),
            (_) => false,
          );
      }
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      setState(() => _error = e.message);
    } catch (_) {
      setState(() => _error = 'Ошибка подключения');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: c.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: c.background,
        elevation: 0,
        iconTheme: IconThemeData(color: c.textPrimary),
        title: Text('Регистрация', style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary)),
      ),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceLg),
          child: switch (_step) {
            _Step.role => _roleStep(c),
            _Step.form => _formStep(c),
            _Step.code => _codeStep(c),
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _roleStep(AppColors c) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Text('Кто вы?',
            style: TextStyle(
                color: c.textPrimary, fontSize: 26, fontWeight: AppTypography.extraBold)),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        Text('Выберите роль — от неё зависит следующий шаг',
            style: TextStyle(color: c.textSecondary, fontSize: AppTypography.body)),
        const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceLg),
        for (final r in _roles)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppDimens.spaceSm),
            child: InkWell(
              onTap: () => _pickRole(r.value),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
              child: Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: c.card,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
                  border: Border.all(color: c.cardBorder),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      width: 44,
                      height: 44,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: c.primary.withOpacity(0.1),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusSm),
                      ),
                      child: Icon(r.icon, color: c.primary),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: AppDimens.spaceMd),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(r.label,
                          style: TextStyle(
                              color: c.textPrimary,
                              fontSize: AppTypography.bodyLarge,
                              fontWeight: AppTypography.semibold)),
                    ),
                    Icon(Icons.chevron_right, color: c.textTertiary),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _formStep(AppColors c) {
    final roleLabel = _roles.firstWhere((r) => r.value == _selectedRole).label;
    return Form(
      key: _formKey,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          TextButton.icon(
            onPressed: () => setState(() => _step = _Step.role),
            icon: Icon(Icons.arrow_back, size: 16, color: c.textSecondary),
            label: Text('$roleLabel — изменить', style: TextStyle(color: c.textSecondary)),
            style: TextButton.styleFrom(padding: EdgeInsets.zero, alignment: Alignment.centerLeft),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
          Text('Ваши данные',
              style: TextStyle(
                  color: c.textPrimary, fontSize: 24, fontWeight: AppTypography.extraBold)),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceLg),
          _field(
            c: c,
            controller: _fullNameCtrl,
            label: 'ФИО',
            hint: 'Иванов Иван Иванович',
            icon: LucideIcons.user,
            validator: (v) {
              final s = v?.trim() ?? '';
              if (s.length < 2) return 'Укажите ФИО';
              return null;
            },
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
          _field(
            c: c,
            controller: _identifierCtrl,
            label: 'Телефон или email',
            hint: '+7 7XX XXX XX XX / email@example.com',
            icon: LucideIcons.atSign,
            keyboard: TextInputType.emailAddress,
            validator: (v) {
              final s = v?.trim() ?? '';
              if (s.isEmpty) return 'Укажите телефон или email';
              final looksEmail = s.contains('@') && s.split('@').last.contains('.');
              final looksPhone = s.replaceAll(RegExp(r'\D'), '').length >= 10;
              if (!looksEmail && !looksPhone) return 'Некорректный телефон или email';
              return null;
            },
          ),
          _telegramBotHint(c),
          if (_error != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
            Text(_error!, style: TextStyle(color: c.danger)),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceLg),
          _submitButton(c, label: 'Получить код', onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _requestCode),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // Постоянная ссылка на бота (REQ-009/telegram-otp.md, срез R2) — заранее открыв бота и нажав
  // «Начать», житель получает код в Telegram мгновенно вместо ожидания SMS.
  Widget _telegramBotHint(AppColors c) {
    final username = dotenv.env['TELEGRAM_BOT_USERNAME'];
    if (username == null || username.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
      child: GestureDetector(
        onTap: () => launchUrl(Uri.parse('https://t.me/$username'),
            mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication),
        child: RichText(
          text: TextSpan(
            text: 'Входите по телефону? Заранее откройте ',
            style: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary, fontSize: 12),
            children: [
              TextSpan(
                text: 'Telegram-бота',
                style: TextStyle(color: c.primary, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
              ),
              const TextSpan(text: ' и нажмите «Начать» — код придёт мгновенно.'),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _codeStep(AppColors c) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Text('Код подтверждения',
            style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary, fontSize: 24, fontWeight: AppTypography.extraBold)),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        Text('Отправлен на $_identifier', style: TextStyle(color: c.textSecondary)),
        const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceLg),
        _field(
          c: c,
          controller: _codeCtrl,
          label: 'Код из письма/SMS',
          hint: '000000',
          icon: LucideIcons.keyRound,
          keyboard: TextInputType.number,
        ),
        if (_devCode != null) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: c.primary.withOpacity(0.12),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
            ),
            child: Text('Код (dev): $_devCode', style: TextStyle(color: c.primaryLight)),
          ),
        ],
        if (_error != null) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
          Text(_error!, style: TextStyle(color: c.danger)),
        ],
        const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceLg),
        _submitButton(c, label: 'Подтвердить', onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _verifyAndRoute),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _submitButton(AppColors c, {required String label, required VoidCallback? onPressed}) {
    return SizedBox(
      width: double.infinity,
      height: AppDimens.primaryTapTarget,
      child: ElevatedButton(
        onPressed: onPressed,
        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
          backgroundColor: c.primary,
          disabledBackgroundColor: c.primary.withOpacity(0.5),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd)),
        ),
        child: _isLoading
            ? const SizedBox(
                width: 22,
                height: 22,
                child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2.5),
              )
            : Text(label,
                style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: AppTypography.bold)),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _field({
    required AppColors c,
    required TextEditingController controller,
    required String label,
    required String hint,
    required IconData icon,
    TextInputType keyboard = TextInputType.text,
    String? Function(String?)? validator,
  }) {
    return TextFormField(
      controller: controller,
      keyboardType: keyboard,
      validator: validator,
      style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary),
      cursorColor: c.primary,
      decoration: InputDecoration(
        labelText: label,
        hintText: hint,
        labelStyle: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary),
        prefixIcon: Icon(icon, color: c.primary, size: 19),
        filled: true,
        fillColor: c.surfaceVariant,
        border: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
          borderSide: BorderSide(color: c.cardBorder),
        ),
        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
          borderSide: BorderSide(color: c.cardBorder),
        ),
        focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
          borderSide: BorderSide(color: c.primary, width: 1.5),
        ),
        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 14),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/requests_repository.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/request_labels.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/events_repository.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/screens/create_request_screen.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/screens/request_thread_screen.dart';

// Мои заявки (mobile-app.md §4, срез 22; живые обновления вместо поллинга — срез 24, REQ-133).
class RequestsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const RequestsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<RequestsScreen> createState() => _RequestsScreenState();
}

class _RequestsScreenState extends State<RequestsScreen> {
  List<ServiceRequest> _items = [];
  bool _loading = true;
  String? _error;
  StreamSubscription<SseEvent>? _sub;
  Timer? _debounce;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
    _sub = EventsRepository.instance.stream.listen((e) {
      if (e.entityType == 'request') {
        _debounce?.cancel();
        _debounce = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 500), _load);
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    _sub?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    setState(() {
      _loading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final items = await RequestsRepository.instance.listMine();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _items = items;
        _loading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _error = e.toString();
        _loading = false;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _createNew() async {
    final id = await Navigator.push<String>(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const CreateRequestScreen()),
    );
    if (id != null && mounted) _load();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: c.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: c.background,
        elevation: 0,
        title: Text('Мои заявки', style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary)),
        iconTheme: IconThemeData(color: c.textPrimary),
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton(
        onPressed: _createNew,
        backgroundColor: c.primary,
        child: const Icon(Icons.add, color: Colors.white),
      ),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: _load,
        child: _loading
            ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
            : _error != null
                ? Center(
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceLg),
                      child: Column(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          Text(_error!, style: TextStyle(color: c.danger), textAlign: TextAlign.center),
                          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
                          OutlinedButton(onPressed: _load, child: const Text('Повторить')),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  )
                : _items.isEmpty
                    ? Center(
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceLg),
                          child: Text(
                            'Заявок пока нет — нажмите «+», чтобы создать первую',
                            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                            style: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary, fontSize: AppTypography.body),
                          ),
                        ),
                      )
                    : ListView.separated(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                            AppDimens.spaceMd, AppDimens.spaceMd, AppDimens.spaceMd, 80),
                        itemCount: _items.length,
                        separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
                        itemBuilder: (_, i) {
                          final r = _items[i];
                          final color = r.status == 'RESOLVED'
                              ? c.success
                              : r.status == 'IN_PROGRESS'
                                  ? c.warning
                                  : c.textTertiary;
                          return InkWell(
                            onTap: () async {
                              await Navigator.push(
                                context,
                                MaterialPageRoute(
                                  builder: (_) => RequestThreadScreen(requestId: r.id),
                                ),
                              );
                              if (mounted) _load();
                            },
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
                            child: Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: c.card,
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
                                border: Border.all(color: c.cardBorder),
                              ),
                              child: Row(
                                children: [
                                  Expanded(
                                    child: Column(
                                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                      children: [
                                        Text(requestCategoryLabels[r.category] ?? r.category,
                                            style: TextStyle(
                                                color: c.textPrimary,
                                                fontSize: AppTypography.cardTitle,
                                                fontWeight: AppTypography.semibold)),
                                        const SizedBox(height: 4),
                                        Text(r.text,
                                            maxLines: 1,
                                            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                            style: TextStyle(color: c.textSecondary)),
                                        const SizedBox(height: 4),
                                        Text(
                                          DateFormat('d MMMM', 'ru').format(r.createdAt.toLocal()),
                                          style:
                                              TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary, fontSize: AppTypography.caption),
                                        ),
                                      ],
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  Container(
                                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 5),
                                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                      color: color.withOpacity(0.12),
                                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
                                    ),
                                    child: Text(
                                      requestStatusLabels[r.status] ?? r.status,
                                      style: TextStyle(
                                          color: color,
                                          fontSize: AppTypography.caption,
                                          fontWeight: AppTypography.semibold),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                          );
                        },
                      ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

=== ФАЙЛ: lib\screens\request_thread_screen.dart ===

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/api_client.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/auth_repository.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/requests_repository.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/request_labels.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/events_repository.dart';

// Заявка + тред (mobile-app.md §4, срез 22) — REQ-003: переписка автора заявки и председателя.
// Живой тред (срез 24, REQ-133): подписка на SSE-события этой заявки — сообщение собеседника
// появляется без ручного обновления; кнопка обновления и авто-подгрузка после отправки остаются
// как резерв на случай разрыва SSE-соединения.
class RequestThreadScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final String requestId;
  const RequestThreadScreen({super.key, required this.requestId});

  @override
  State<RequestThreadScreen> createState() => _RequestThreadScreenState();
}

const Map<String, Color Function(AppColors)> _statusColor = {
  'RECEIVED': _grey,
  'IN_PROGRESS': _warn,
  'RESOLVED': _ok,
};
Color _grey(AppColors c) => c.textTertiary;
Color _warn(AppColors c) => c.warning;
Color _ok(AppColors c) => c.success;

class _RequestThreadScreenState extends State<RequestThreadScreen> {
  ServiceRequest? _request;
  bool _loading = true;
  String? _error;
  String? _myUserId;
  final _msgCtrl = TextEditingController();
  bool _sending = false;
  StreamSubscription<SseEvent>? _sub;
  Timer? _debounce;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
    _sub = EventsRepository.instance.stream.listen((e) {
      if (e.entityType == 'request' && e.entityId == widget.requestId) {
        _debounce?.cancel();
        _debounce = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 500), _load);
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    _sub?.cancel();
    _msgCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    setState(() {
      _loading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final results = await Future.wait([
        RequestsRepository.instance.get(widget.requestId),
        AuthRepository.instance.me(),
      ]);
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _request = results[0] as ServiceRequest;
        _myUserId = (results[1] as AuthUser?)?.id;
        _loading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _error = e.toString();
        _loading = false;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _send() async {
    final text = _msgCtrl.text.trim();
    if (text.isEmpty) return;
    setState(() => _sending = true);
    try {
      await RequestsRepository.instance.addMessage(widget.requestId, text);
      _msgCtrl.clear();
      await _load();
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(e.message)));
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _sending = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: c.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: c.background,
        elevation: 0,
        title: Text(
          _request != null ? (requestCategoryLabels[_request!.category] ?? _request!.category) : 'Заявка',
          style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary),
        ),
        iconTheme: IconThemeData(color: c.textPrimary),
        actions: [
          IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.refresh, color: c.textPrimary), onPressed: _load),
        ],
      ),
      body: _loading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : _error != null
              ? Center(
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceLg),
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Text(_error!, style: TextStyle(color: c.danger), textAlign: TextAlign.center),
                        const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
                        OutlinedButton(onPressed: _load, child: const Text('Повторить')),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                )
              : Column(
                  children: [
                    _StatusBar(request: _request!, colors: c),
                    Expanded(
                      child: ListView(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
                        children: [
                          Container(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: c.card,
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
                              border: Border.all(color: c.cardBorder),
                            ),
                            child: Text(_request!.text, style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary)),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
                          for (final m in _request!.messages)
                            _MessageBubble(
                              message: m,
                              isMine: m.authorUserId == _myUserId,
                              colors: c,
                            ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    _Composer(controller: _msgCtrl, sending: _sending, onSend: _send, colors: c),
                  ],
                ),
    );
  }
}

class _StatusBar extends StatelessWidget {
  final ServiceRequest request;
  final AppColors colors;
  const _StatusBar({required this.request, required this.colors});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = colors;
    final color = (_statusColor[request.status] ?? _grey)(c);
    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: AppDimens.spaceMd, vertical: AppDimens.spaceSm),
      color: color.withOpacity(0.1),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(width: 8, height: 8, decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color, shape: BoxShape.circle)),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Text(requestStatusLabels[request.status] ?? request.status,
              style: TextStyle(color: color, fontWeight: AppTypography.semibold)),
          const Spacer(),
          Text(DateFormat('d MMMM', 'ru').format(request.createdAt.toLocal()),
              style: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary, fontSize: AppTypography.caption)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _MessageBubble extends StatelessWidget {
  final RequestMessage message;
  final bool isMine;
  final AppColors colors;
  const _MessageBubble({required this.message, required this.isMine, required this.colors});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = colors;
    return Align(
      alignment: isMine ? Alignment.centerRight : Alignment.centerLeft,
      child: Container(
        margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppDimens.spaceSm),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: AppDimens.spaceMd, vertical: 10),
        constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 280),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: isMine ? c.primary : c.surfaceVariant,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
        ),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(message.text, style: TextStyle(color: isMine ? Colors.white : c.textPrimary)),
            const SizedBox(height: 4),
            Text(
              DateFormat('HH:mm').format(message.createdAt.toLocal()),
              style: TextStyle(
                color: isMine ? Colors.white70 : c.textTertiary,
                fontSize: 11,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _Composer extends StatelessWidget {
  final TextEditingController controller;
  final bool sending;
  final VoidCallback onSend;
  final AppColors colors;
  const _Composer({
    required this.controller,
    required this.sending,
    required this.onSend,
    required this.colors,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = colors;
    return SafeArea(
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceSm),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(color: c.card, border: Border(top: BorderSide(color: c.divider))),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: TextField(
                controller: controller,
                style: TextStyle(color: c.textPrimary),
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  hintText: 'Сообщение…',
                  hintStyle: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary),
                  filled: true,
                  fillColor: c.surfaceVariant,
                  contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
                  border: OutlineInputBorder(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
                    borderSide: BorderSide.none,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: AppDimens.spaceSm),
            IconButton(
              onPressed: sending ? null : onSend,
              icon: sending
                  ? SizedBox(
                      width: 20,
                      height: 20,
                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2, color: c.primary),
                    )
                  : Icon(Icons.send, color: c.primary),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:lucide_icons/lucide_icons.dart';

// Оболочка жителя (mobile-app.md §4, срез 22; таб «Голоса» — срез 23) — заменяет
// main_wrapper.dart/resident_home_page.dart для роли RESIDENT. Таб-бар: Главная / Голоса /
// Заявки / Финансы / Профиль (Объявления и Инфо — с главной и из профиля, не отдельные табы).
class ResidentHomeShell extends StatefulWidget {
  final AuthUser user;
  const ResidentHomeShell({super.key, required this.user});

  @override
  State<ResidentHomeShell> createState() => _ResidentHomeShellState();
}

class _ResidentHomeShellState extends State<ResidentHomeShell> {
  int _tab = 0;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    // Живые обновления (срез 24) — один коннект на всё время сессии жителя в приложении,
    // разрывается при выходе на LoginPage (эта оболочка тогда уходит с дерева виджетов).
    EventsRepository.instance.connect();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    EventsRepository.instance.disconnect();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    final pages = [
      _HomeTab(user: widget.user),
      const PollsScreen(),
      const RequestsScreen(),
      const FinanceScreen(),
      _ProfileTab(user: widget.user),
    ];

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: c.background,
      body: IndexedStack(index: _tab, children: pages),
      bottomNavigationBar: NavigationBarTheme(
        data: NavigationBarThemeData(
          backgroundColor: c.card,
          indicatorColor: c.primary.withOpacity(0.15),
          labelTextStyle: WidgetStateProperty.resolveWith((states) {
            final selected = states.contains(WidgetState.selected);
            return TextStyle(
              fontSize: AppTypography.caption,
              color: selected ? c.primary : c.textTertiary,
              fontWeight: selected ? AppTypography.semibold : AppTypography.regular,
            );
          }),
          iconTheme: WidgetStateProperty.resolveWith((states) {
            final selected = states.contains(WidgetState.selected);
            return IconThemeData(color: selected ? c.primary : c.textTertiary);
          }),
        ),
        child: NavigationBar(
          selectedIndex: _tab,
          onDestinationSelected: (i) => setState(() => _tab = i),
          destinations: const [
            NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(LucideIcons.home), label: 'Главная'),
            NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(LucideIcons.vote), label: 'Голоса'),
            NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(LucideIcons.clipboardList), label: 'Заявки'),
            NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(LucideIcons.wallet), label: 'Финансы'),
            NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(LucideIcons.user), label: 'Профиль'),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _HomeTab extends StatefulWidget {
  final AuthUser user;
  const _HomeTab({required this.user});

  @override
  State<_HomeTab> createState() => _HomeTabState();
}

class _HomeTabState extends State<_HomeTab> {
  List<Announcement> _recent = [];
  bool _loading = true;
  StreamSubscription<SseEvent>? _sub;
  Timer? _debounce;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
    _sub = EventsRepository.instance.stream.listen((e) {
      if (e.entityType == 'announcement') {
        _debounce?.cancel();
        _debounce = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 500), _load);
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    _sub?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    try {
      final all = await AnnouncementsRepository.instance.list();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _recent = all.take(3).toList();
        _loading = false;
      });
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _loading = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: c.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: c.background,
        elevation: 0,
        title: const Text('Fixly'),
        titleTextStyle: TextStyle(
            color: c.textPrimary, fontSize: AppTypography.screenTitle, fontWeight: AppTypography.bold),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: Icon(LucideIcons.bell, color: c.textPrimary),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.push(
              context,
              MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const NotificationsScreen()),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: _load,
        child: ListView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
          children: [
            _QuickLinks(colors: c),
            const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceLg),
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Text('Объявления',
                    style: TextStyle(
                        color: c.textPrimary,
                        fontSize: AppTypography.sectionTitle,
                        fontWeight: AppTypography.bold)),
                TextButton(
                  onPressed: () => Navigator.push(
                    context,
                    MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const AnnouncementsScreen()),
                  ),
                  child: Text('Все', style: TextStyle(color: c.primary)),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceSm),
            if (_loading)
              const Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
                child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
              )
            else if (_recent.isEmpty)
              Text('Объявлений пока нет', style: TextStyle(color: c.textTertiary))
            else
              for (final a in _recent)
                Container(
                  margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppDimens.spaceSm),
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: c.card,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
                    border: Border.all(color: c.cardBorder),
                  ),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(a.title,
                          style: TextStyle(
                              color: c.textPrimary, fontWeight: AppTypography.semibold)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Text(a.body,
                          maxLines: 2,
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                          style: TextStyle(color: c.textSecondary)),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _QuickLinks extends StatelessWidget {
  final AppColors colors;
  const _QuickLinks({required this.colors});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = colors;
    final items = [
      (LucideIcons.info, 'Инфо о доме', () => const InfoScreen()),
    ];

      children: items
          .map((it) => Expanded(
                child: InkWell(
                  onTap: () => Navigator.push(
                    context,
                    MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => it.$3()),
                  ),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
                  child: Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: c.card,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
                      border: Border.all(color: c.cardBorder),
                    ),
                    child: Column(
                      children: [
                        Icon(it.$1, color: c.primary),
                        const SizedBox(height: 6),
                        Text(it.$2,
                            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                            style: TextStyle(color: c.textSecondary, fontSize: AppTypography.caption)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ))
          .toList(),
    );
  }
}

class _ProfileTab extends StatelessWidget {
  final AuthUser user;
  const _ProfileTab({required this.user});

  Future<void> _logout(BuildContext context) async {
    await AuthRepository.instance.logout();
    if (!context.mounted) return;
    Navigator.pushAndRemoveUntil(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const LoginPage()),
      (_) => false,
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);

          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppDimens.spaceMd),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: c.card,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimens.radiusMd),
              border: Border.all(color: c.cardBorder),
            ),

              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,

                  _Row(icon: LucideIcons.mail, label: user.email!, colors: c),

                  _Row(icon: LucideIcons.phone, label: user.phone!, colors: c),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
          _MenuTile(
            icon: LucideIcons.info,
            label: 'Инфо о доме',
            colors: c,
            onTap: () => Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const InfoScreen())),

          _MenuTile(
            icon: LucideIcons.bell,
            label: 'Уведомления',
            colors: c,
            onTap: () =>
                Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const NotificationsScreen())),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceLg),
          const FeatureCatalogSection(isChairman: false),
          const SizedBox(height: AppDimens.spaceMd),
          SizedBox(
            height: AppDimens.primaryTapTarget,
            child: OutlinedButton.icon(
              onPressed: () => _logout(context),
              icon: Icon(LucideIcons.logOut, color: c.danger),
              label: Text('Выйти', style: TextStyle(color: c.danger)),
              style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(side: BorderSide(color: c.danger.withOpacity(0.4))),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

=========================================
=== ФАЙЛ: lib\screens\splash_screen.dart ===
=========================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/core/auth_repository.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/screens/start_screen.dart';
import 'package:fixly_app/screens/home_router.dart';

// ============================================================
//  SplashScreen — первый экран при запуске (палитра — U3, срез R2)
//  • Логотип с анимацией появления
//  • Пульсирующее кольцо
//  • Текстовый слоган
//  • Автоматический редирект (авторизован → HomeRouter, нет → StartScreen)
// ============================================================
class SplashScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const SplashScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<SplashScreen> createState() => _SplashScreenState();
}

class _SplashScreenState extends State<SplashScreen>
    with TickerProviderStateMixin {

  // Контроллеры анимаций
  late AnimationController _logoCtrl;
  late AnimationController _pulseCtrl;
  late AnimationController _textCtrl;
  late AnimationController _bgCtrl;

  // Логотип
  late Animation<double>  _logoScale;
  late Animation<double>  _logoOpacity;

  // Пульс
  late Animation<double>  _pulseScale;
  late Animation<double>  _pulseOpacity;

  // Текст
  late Animation<double>  _textOpacity;
  late Animation<Offset>  _textSlide;

  // Фон
  late Animation<double>  _bgOpacity;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _setupAnimations();
    _startSequence();
  }

  void _setupAnimations() {
    // Фон — появляется сразу
    _bgCtrl = AnimationController(
        vsync: this, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 600));
    _bgOpacity = CurvedAnimation(parent: _bgCtrl, curve: Curves.easeIn);

    // Логотип — выпрыгивает с упругостью
    _logoCtrl = AnimationController(
        vsync: this, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 900));
    _logoScale = Tween<double>(begin: 0.0, end: 1.0).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(parent: _logoCtrl, curve: Curves.elasticOut),
    );
    _logoOpacity = Tween<double>(begin: 0.0, end: 1.0).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(
          parent: _logoCtrl,
          curve: const Interval(0.0, 0.4, curve: Curves.easeIn)),
    );

    // Пульс вокруг логотипа — бесконечная петля
    _pulseCtrl = AnimationController(
        vsync: this, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 1800));
    _pulseScale = Tween<double>(begin: 1.0, end: 1.8).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(parent: _pulseCtrl, curve: Curves.easeOut),
    );
    _pulseOpacity = Tween<double>(begin: 0.35, end: 0.0).animate(
      CurvedAnimation(parent: _pulseCtrl, curve: Curves.easeOut),
    );

    // Текст — всплывает снизу
    _textCtrl = AnimationController(
        vsync: this, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 700));
    _textOpacity = CurvedAnimation(
        parent: _textCtrl, curve: Curves.easeIn);
    _textSlide = Tween<Offset>(
            begin: const Offset(0, 0.5), end: Offset.zero)
        .animate(CurvedAnimation(
            parent: _textCtrl, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic));
  }

  Future<void> _startSequence() async {
    // 1. Фон
    _bgCtrl.forward();
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 200));

    // 2. Логотип
    _logoCtrl.forward();
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 400));

    // 3. Пульс (бесконечно)
    _pulseCtrl.repeat();

    // 4. Текст
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));
    _textCtrl.forward();

    // 5. Держим сплэш + проверяем сессию
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 1800));
    await _checkSessionAndNavigate();
  }

  Future<void> _checkSessionAndNavigate() async {
    // Срез 21 (ADR-0012 §1): сессия — Bearer-токен в защищённом хранилище, не Supabase.
    // me() возвращает null и чистит токен сам, если сессия истекла/отозвана на сервере.
    // Любой сбой сети (офлайн, сервер лежит, таймаут ApiClient) — НЕ повод зависнуть на
    // сплэше: уходим на StartScreen, токен при этом не трогаем (code review 2026-07-16).
    // Полноценный офлайн-режим с кешем профиля — предмет среза 22, не сплэша.
    AuthUser? user;
    try {
      user = await AuthRepository.instance.me();
    } catch (_) {
      user = null;
    }

    if (!mounted) return;

    Widget destination;
    if (user != null) {
      destination = HomeRouter(user: user);
    } else {
      destination = const StartScreen();
    }

    Navigator.pushReplacement(
      context,
      PageRouteBuilder(
        pageBuilder: (_, anim, __) => destination,
        transitionsBuilder: (_, anim, __, child) => FadeTransition(
          opacity: anim,
          child: child,
        ),
        transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 500),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _logoCtrl.dispose();
    _pulseCtrl.dispose();
    _textCtrl.dispose();
    _bgCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = AppColors.of(context);
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: c.background,
      body: FadeTransition(
        opacity: _bgOpacity,
        child: Container(
          width: double.infinity,
          height: double.infinity,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            gradient: LinearGradient(
              begin: Alignment.topLeft,
              end: Alignment.bottomRight,
              colors: [c.background, c.surface, c.background],
              stops: const [0.0, 0.5, 1.0],
            ),
          ),
          child: Stack(
            children: [
              // Декоративные точки фона
              ..._buildBackgroundDots(c),

              // Центральный контент
              Center(
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    // Логотип с пульсом
                    Stack(
                      alignment: Alignment.center,
                      children: [
                        // Внешний пульс
                        AnimatedBuilder(
                          animation: _pulseCtrl,
                          builder: (_, __) => Opacity(
                            opacity: _pulseOpacity.value,
                            child: Transform.scale(
                              scale: _pulseScale.value,
                              child: Container(
                                width: 110,
                                height: 110,
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                  border: Border.all(
                                    color: c.primary,
                                    width: 2,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),

                        // Логотип
                        AnimatedBuilder(
                          animation: _logoCtrl,
                          builder: (_, child) => Opacity(
                            opacity: _logoOpacity.value,
                            child: Transform.scale(
                              scale: _logoScale.value,
                              child: child,
                            ),
                          ),
                          child: Container(
                            width: 100,
                            height: 100,
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              shape: BoxShape.circle,
                              gradient: LinearGradient(
                                begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                                end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                                colors: [c.gradientStart, c.gradientEnd],
                              ),
                              boxShadow: [
                                BoxShadow(
                                  color: c.primary.withOpacity(0.5),
                                  blurRadius: 30,
                                  spreadRadius: 5,
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                            child: const Center(
                              child: Text(
                                'F',
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: Colors.white,
                                  fontSize: 52,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                                  letterSpacing: -2,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),

                    const SizedBox(height: 32),

                    // Название + слоган
                    SlideTransition(
                      position: _textSlide,
                      child: FadeTransition(
                        opacity: _textOpacity,
                        child: Column(
                          children: [
                            Text(
                              'Fixly',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: c.textPrimary,
                                fontSize: 42,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                                letterSpacing: -1.5,
                              ),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 8),
                            Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                  horizontal: 16, vertical: 6),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: c.primary.withOpacity(0.15),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                                border: Border.all(
                                  color: c.primary.withOpacity(0.3),
                                ),
                              ),
                              child: Text(
                                'Платформа вашего дома',
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: c.primaryLight,
                                  fontSize: 14,
                                  letterSpacing: 0.5,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),

              // Версия внизу
              Positioned(
                bottom: 40,
                left: 0,
                right: 0,
                child: FadeTransition(
                  opacity: _textOpacity,
                  child: Text(
                    'v1.0.0',
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: c.textTertiary,
                      fontSize: 14,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  List<Widget> _buildBackgroundDots(AppColors c) {
    final positions = [
      [0.1, 0.15], [0.85, 0.1], [0.05, 0.7],
      [0.9, 0.65], [0.5, 0.08], [0.75, 0.85],
      [0.2, 0.9],  [0.95, 0.4],
    ];
    final sizes = [4.0, 3.0, 5.0, 3.0, 4.0, 3.5, 4.5, 3.0];

    return List.generate(positions.length, (i) {
      return Positioned(
        left:
            MediaQuery.of(context).size.width * positions[i][0],
        top: MediaQuery.of(context).size.height * positions[i][1],
        child: AnimatedBuilder(
          animation: _pulseCtrl,
          builder: (_, __) => Opacity(
            opacity: 0.15 +
                (_pulseCtrl.value * 0.15 * (i % 2 == 0 ? 1 : -1))
                    .clamp(0.0, 0.3),
            child: Container(
              width: sizes[i],
              height: sizes[i],
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: c.primary,
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      );
    });
  }
}
